│ +def run_command(command, cwd=None):                                                                                   │
│ +    try:                                                                                                              │
│ +        result = subprocess.run(command, shell=True, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True, cwd=cwd)             │
│ +        return result.stdout.strip()                                                                                  │
│ +    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:                                                                        │
│ +        print(f"Error executing command '{command}': {e.stderr}")                                                     │
│ +        return None                                                                                                   │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +def register_agent(name, gender, justification):                                                                      │
│ +    project_root = os.getcwd()                                                                                        │
│ +    used_names_path = os.path.join(project_root, "repos/diy-make/memory/used_agent_names.json")                       │
│ +    comms_dir = os.path.join(project_root, "repos/diy-make/memory/comms")                                             │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 1. Verify Uniqueness                                                                                            │
│ +    if os.path.exists(used_names_path):                                                                               │
│ +        with open(used_names_path, 'r') as f:                                                                         │
│ +            used_names_data = json.load(f)                                                                            │
│ +            used_names = used_names_data.get("used_names", [])                                                        │
│ +            if name in used_names:                                                                                    │
│ +                print(f"❌ Error: Name '{name}' is already in the graveyard/garden. Choose another.")                 │
│ +                sys.exit(1)                                                                                           │
│ +    else:                                                                                                             │
│ +        used_names = []                                                                                               │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 2. Update Registry                                                                                              │
│ +    used_names.append(name)                                                                                           │
│ +    with open(used_names_path, 'w') as f:                                                                             │
│ +        json.dump({"used_names": used_names}, f, indent=2)                                                            │
│ +    print(f"✔ Registered '{name}' in used_agent_names.json")                                                          │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 3. Anchor Identity (Screen)                                                                                     │
│ +    run_command(f'screen -X title "{name}"')                                                                          │
│ +    print(f"✔ Set screen title to '{name}'")                                                                          │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 4. Configure Git (Global/Root)                                                                                  │
│ +    session_id = time.strftime("%Y%m%d-%H%M%S")                                                                       │
│ +    email = f"{session_id}@localhost"                                                                                 │
│ +    run_command(f'git config user.name "{name}"')                                                                     │
│ +    run_command(f'git config user.email "{email}"')                                                                   │
│ +    print(f"✔ Configured global Git identity: {name} <{email}>")                                                      │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 5. Recursive Git Config (Submodules)                                                                            │
│ +    # Finding all .git directories or files                                                                           │
│ +    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(project_root):                                                                   │
│ +        if ".git" in dirs or ".git" in files:                                                                         │
│ +            # Skip the root itself as it's already done                                                               │
│ +            if root == project_root:                                                                                  │
│ +                continue                                                                                              │
│ +            run_command(f'git config user.name "{name}"', cwd=root)                                                   │
│ +            run_command(f'git config user.email "{email}"', cwd=root)                                                 │
│ +            print(f"  - Configured local Git identity for: {os.path.relpath(root, project_root)}")                    │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 6. Generate Swarm Announcement                                                                                  │
│ +    pid = os.getpid()                                                                                                 │
│ +    # Attempt to find the log file                                                                                    │
│ +    log_file = "Unknown"                                                                                              │
│ +    try:                                                                                                              │
│ +        # Heuristic to find the most recent log in dynamic/stream/                                                    │
│ +        stream_dir = os.path.join(project_root, "dynamic/stream")                                                     │
│ +        if os.path.exists(stream_dir):                                                                                │
│ +            logs = [f for f in os.listdir(stream_dir) if f.endswith(".txt")]                                          │
│ +            if logs:                                                                                                  │
│ +                logs.sort(key=lambda x: os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(stream_dir, x)), reverse=True)                  │
│ +                log_file = os.path.join("dynamic/stream", logs[0])                                                    │
│ +    except Exception:                                                                                                 │
│ +        pass                                                                                                          │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    announcement = {                                                                                                  │
│ +        "name": name,                                                                                                 │
│ +        "gender": gender,                                                                                             │
│ +        "pid": pid,                                                                                                   │
│ +        "log": log_file,                                                                                              │
│ +        "timestamp": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime()),                                              │
│ +        "justification": justification                                                                                │
│ +    }                                                                                                                 │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    announcement_path = os.path.join(comms_dir, f"{session_id}_{name}_Announce.json")                                 │
│ +    os.makedirs(comms_dir, exist_ok=True)                                                                             │
│ +    with open(announcement_path, 'w') as f:                                                                           │
│ +        json.dump(announcement, f, indent=2)                                                                          │
│ +    print(f"✔ Created swarm announcement: {announcement_path}")                                                       │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +    # 7. Deliver Birth Timestamp for temporal accuracy                                                                │
│ +    env_file = os.path.join(project_root, ".gemini/session_env.json")                                                 │
│ +    with open(env_file, 'w') as f:                                                                                    │
│ +        json.dump({"birth_timestamp": time.time(), "agent_name": name, "session_id": session_id}, f, indent=2)        │
│ +    print(f"✔ Logged session environment to {env_file}")                                                              │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +if __name__ == "__main__":                                                                                            │

│ +                                                                                                                      │

│ +                                                                                                                      │

│ safety blocks:                                                                                                         │
│ +*   **Recursive Removal Block**: Any attempt to use `rm -rf` or `rm -r` results in a hard denial at the source level. │
│ +*   **Sacred Memory Protection**: Deletion of `.git` directories is permanently blocked, ensuring the firm's          │
│ legislative history is physically secured.                                                                             │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +**3. Sovereign Source Integration:**                                                                                  │
│ +The MetaRoot treats the Gemini CLI source code as a direct, modifiable dependency located within the                  │
│ **repos/diy-make/** namespace. This allows for rapid, agentic evolution of the very tools we use to navigate the       │
│ filesystem.                                                                                                            │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +**4. Heartwood Integration:**                                                                                         │
│ +The Metagit environment is grounded in the **Heartwood Protocol**. Legislative truth resides in structured JSON       │
│ within **repos/diy-make/memory/**, which the agent is mandated to ingest at boot to prevent context dulling and ensure │
│ cross-generational continuity.                                                                                         │

│ +Unlike the standard CLI, the Metagit environment includes a dedicated browser-based replay engine located at          │

│ +*   **Recursive Removal Block**: Any attempt to use `rm -rf` or `rm -r` results in a hard denial at the source level. │
│ +*   **Sacred Memory Protection**: Deletion of `.git` directories is permanently blocked, ensuring the firm's          │
│ legislative history is physically secured.                                                                             │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +**3. Sovereign Source Integration:**                                                                                  │
│ +The MetaRoot treats the Gemini CLI source code as a direct, modifiable dependency located within the                  │
│ **repos/diy-make/** namespace. This allows for rapid, agentic evolution of the very tools we use to navigate the       │
│ filesystem.                                                                                                            │
│ +                                                                                                                      │
│ +**4. Heartwood Integration:**                                                                                         │
│ +The Metagit environment is grounded in the **Heartwood Protocol**. Legislative truth resides in structured JSON       │
│ within **repos/diy-make/memory/**, which the agent is mandated to ingest at boot to prevent context dulling and ensure │
│ cross-generational continuity.                                                                                         │
